And all the time Thery smoked cigarettes, looking into the fire with his head on his hands.

“Going back to this matter we have on our hands,” said Gonsalez: “I suppose that there is nothing more to be done till﻿—the day?”

“Nothing.”

===Chinese===
这半天里特里一直抽着烟，双手支头，望着炉火。

「回到咱们手上这件事来，」冈萨雷斯说，「我想在那天之前，再没有别的可做了吧？」

「没有了。」

===Original===
“And after?”

“There are our fine art reproductions.”

“And after?” persisted Poiccart.

“There is a case in Holland, Hermannus van der Byl, to wit; but it will be simple, and there will be no necessity to warn.”

===Chinese===
「之后呢？」

「咱们还有美术复制品的营生。」

「再之后呢？」波伊卡特追问。

「荷兰有一桩案子，就是赫尔曼努斯·范德拜尔（Hermannus van der Byl）；不过手到擒来，无须预告。」

===Original===
Poiccart’s face was grave.

“I am glad you have suggested van der Byl, he should have been dealt with before﻿—Hook of Holland or Flushing?”

“If we have time, the Hook by all means.”

===Chinese===
波伊卡特面色郑重起来。

「你提范德拜尔，我很高兴，他早该料理了——荷兰角，还是弗利辛恩？」

「来得及的话，就走荷兰角。」

===Original===
“And Thery?”

“I will see to him,” said Gonsalez easily; “we will go overland to Jerez﻿—where the girl is,” he added laughingly.

The object of their discussion finished his tenth cigarette and sat up in his chair with a grunt.

===Chinese===
「那特里呢？」

「交给我，」冈萨雷斯轻巧地说，「我们走陆路去赫雷斯——姑娘在那儿呢。」他笑着添了一句。

他们议论的当事人才抽完第十支烟，哼了一声，在椅子里坐直了。

===Original===
“I forgot to tell you,” Leon went on, “that today, when we were taking our exercise walk, Thery was considerably interested in the posters he saw everywhere, and was particularly curious to know why so many people were reading them. I had to find a lie on the spur of the minute, and I hate lying”﻿—Gonsalez was perfectly sincere. “I invented a story about racing or lotteries or something of the sort, and he was satisfied.”

Thery had caught his name in spite of its Anglicized pronunciation, and looked inquiry.

“We will leave you to amuse our friend,” said Manfred, rising. “Poiccart and I have a few experiments to make.”

===Chinese===
「忘了告诉你们，」莱昂接着说，「今天我们照例散步的时候，特里对满街的告示大感兴趣，尤其好奇怎么那么多人都围着看。我只好当场现编一个谎，而我最恨撒谎」——冈萨雷斯说的是十足的真话。「我编了个赛马啦彩票啦之类的故事，他信了。」

名字尽管被念成了英语腔，特里还是捕捉到了，投来探问的一眼。

「咱们的朋友就交给你逗乐了。」曼弗雷德起身说。「我和波伊卡特要做几样实验。」

===Original===
The two left the room, traversed the narrow passage, and paused before a small door at the end. A larger door on the right, padlocked and barred, led to the studio. Drawing a small key from his pocket, Manfred opened the door, and stepping into the room switched on a light that shone dimly through a dust-covered bulb. There had been some attempt at restoring order from the chaos. Two shelves had been cleared of rubbish, and on these stood rows of bright little phials, each bearing a number. A rough table had been pushed against the wall beneath the shelves, and on the green baize with which the table was covered was a litter of graduated measures, test-tubes, condensors, delicate scales, and two queer-shaped glass machines, not unlike gas generators.

Poiccart pulled a chair to the table, and gingerly lifted a metal cup that stood in a dish of water. Manfred, looking over his shoulder, remarked on the consistency of the liquid that half-filled the vessel, and Poiccart bent his head, acknowledging the remark as though it were a compliment.

===Chinese===
两人出了屋，穿过窄窄的过道，在尽头一扇小门前站定。右手一扇更大的门，挂着挂锁、闩着门闩，通向摄影棚。曼弗雷德从口袋里摸出一把小钥匙开了门，进屋拧亮灯，灯光透过积尘的灯泡，昏昏地照着。乱局里已见出几分收拾的眉目：两层架子清出了杂物，摆着一排排亮晶晶的小药瓶，只只编着号。一张粗木桌推在架子底下的墙边，桌面的绿呢上摊着量杯、试管、冷凝器、精密天平，还有两台模样古怪的玻璃家伙，与煤气发生炉颇有几分相像。

波伊卡特把椅子拉到桌前，小心翼翼端起一只立在水盘里的金属杯。曼弗雷德从他肩后看去，品评着杯中半盈液体的稠度，波伊卡特微微颔首，把这话当恭维领受了。

===Original===
“Yes,” he said, satisfied, “it is a complete success; the formula is quite right. Some day we may want to use this.”

He replaced the cup in its bath, and, reaching beneath the table, produced from a pail a handful of ice-dust, with which he carefully surrounded the receptacle.

“I regard that as the *multum in parvo* of explosives,” he said, and took down a small phial from the shelf, lifted the stopper with the crook of his little finger, and poured a few drops of a whitish liquid into the metal cup.

===Chinese===
「成了，」他满意地说，「大功告成；配方分毫不差。哪天兴许用得上它。」

他把杯子放回水浴，又探到桌下，从一只桶里抓出一把冰屑，细细围住那容器。

「我把这东西看作炸药中的 *multum in parvo*（小中见大）。」他说着，从架上取下一只小药瓶，小指一勾起了塞子，往金属杯里倾了几滴发白的液体。

===Original===
“That neutralizes the elements,” said Poiccart, and gave a sigh of relief. “I am not a nervous man, but the present is the first comfortable moment I have had for two days.”

“It makes an abominable smell,” said Manfred, with his handkerchief to his nose.

A thin smoke was rising from the cup.

“I never notice those things,” Poiccart replied, dipping a thin glass rod into the mess. He lifted the rod, and watched reddish drops dripping from the end.

===Chinese===
「这一下就把各元素中和了。」波伊卡特说，舒了一口气。「我并非胆小之人，可眼下这工夫，是我两天来头一回松快。」

「这味儿可真难闻死了。」曼弗雷德说着，手帕掩住鼻子。

一缕细细的烟正从杯中升起。

「这种事我从不留意。」波伊卡特答道，把一根细玻璃棒插进那滩东西里。他提起棒，看着淡红的液滴从棒端一滴滴坠下。

===Original===
“That’s all right,” he said.

“And it is an explosive no more?” asked Manfred.

“It is as harmless as a cup of chocolate.”

Poiccart wiped the rod on a rag, replaced the phial and turned to his companion.

===Chinese===
「成了。」他说。

「它就再不是炸药了？」曼弗雷德问。

「它跟一杯巧克力一样无害。」

波伊卡特把玻璃棒在一块抹布上擦净，放回药瓶，转向同伴。

===Original===
“And now?” he asked.

Manfred made no answer, but unlocked an old-fashioned safe that stood in the corner of the room. From this he removed a box of polished wood. He opened the box and disclosed the contents.

“If Thery is the good workman he says he is, here is the bait that shall lure Sir Philip Ramon to his death,” he said.

===Chinese===
「现在呢？」

曼弗雷德不作答，却去开了屋角一只老式保险柜，从中取出一只磨光的木匣。他打开匣子，亮出里面的东西。

「倘若特里真有他自夸的那般好手艺，这便是引菲利普·拉蒙爵士走向死亡的饵。」他说。

===Original===
Poiccart looked. “Very ingenious,” was his only comment; then﻿—“Does Thery know, quite know, the stir he has created?”

Manfred closed the lid and replaced the box before he replied.

“Does Thery know that he is the fourth Just Man?” he asked; then slowly﻿—“I think not﻿—and it is as well that he does not know; a thousand pounds is roughly thirty-three thousand pesetas, and there is the free pardon﻿—and the girl at Jerez,” he added, thoughtfully.

===Chinese===
波伊卡特看了看。「巧得很。」他只评了这一句；随即——「特里知道吗，当真知道，自己搅起了多大的风浪？」

曼弗雷德合上匣盖，把匣子放回原处，才答话。

「特里可知道，他自己就是第四义士？」他问；随即缓缓道——「我想他不知道——他不知道倒也好；一千英镑约合三万三千比塞塔，外带一纸特赦——还有赫雷斯的那姑娘。」他若有所思地补了一句。

---
===Original===
A brilliant idea came to Smith, the reporter, and he carried it to the chief.

“Not bad,” said the editor, which meant that the idea was really very good﻿—“not bad at all.”

===Chinese===
记者史密斯灵光一现，把主意捧给了主编。

「不坏。」主编说——这话的意思其实是这主意好得很——「一点也不坏。」

===Original===
“It occurred to me,” said the gratified reporter, “that one or two of the four might be foreigners who don’t understand a word of English.”

“Quite so,” said the chief; “thank you for the suggestion. I’ll have it done tonight.”

Which dialogue accounts for the fact that the next morning the *Megaphone* appeared with the police notice printed in French, Italian, German﻿—and Spanish.

===Chinese===
「我想到，」受夸的记者说，「那四个人里，保不齐有一两个是不懂半句英语的外国人。」

「正是。」主编说，「多谢你的高见。今晚就办。」

这段对话解释了一件事：次日早晨的《传声筒报》，把警方告示用法语、意大利语、德语——还有西班牙语——一并刊了出来。

## VI The Outrage at the *Megaphone* / 六 《传声筒报》惊变
===Original===
The editor of the *Megaphone*, returning from dinner, met the super-chief on the stairs. The super-chief, boyish of face, withdrew his mind from the mental contemplation of a new project (“Megaphone House” is the home of new projects) and inquired after the Four Just Men.

“The excitement is keeping up,” replied the editor. “People are talking of nothing else but the coming debate on the Extradition Bill, and the Government is taking every precaution against an attack upon Ramon.”

===Chinese===
《传声筒报》的主编晚饭归来，在楼梯上遇见了大总编。大总编生着一张娃娃脸，把自己那颗心思从一桩新计划的冥想里拽出来（「传声筒报大楼」本就是新计划的安乐窝），问起四义士的近况。

「热度还旺着呢。」主编答道。「人们张口闭口都是引渡法案即将到来的那场辩论，政府正千方百计防备有人对拉蒙下手。」

===Original===
“What is the feeling?”

The editor shrugged his shoulders.

“Nobody really believes that anything will happen, in spite of the bomb.”

===Chinese===
「风向如何？」

主编耸了耸肩。

「虽说出了炸弹那一桩，真信要出事的人，一个也没有。」

===Original===
The super-chief thought for a moment, and then asked, quickly,

“What do *you* think?”

The editor laughed.

“I think the threat will never be fulfilled; for once the Four have struck against a snag. If they hadn’t warned Ramon they might have done something, but forewarned﻿—”

“We shall see,” said the super-chief, and went home.

===Chinese===
大总编想了一想，随即飞快地问：「*你*怎么看？」

主编笑了。

「我看这威胁永远兑不了现；这一回，四义士算是撞上暗礁了。他们若不曾预先警告拉蒙，没准还成得了事，可是先打了招呼——」

「走着瞧吧。」大总编说罢回家去了。

===Original===
The editor wondered, as he climbed the stairs, how much longer the Four would fill the contents bill of his newspaper, and rather hoped that they would make their attempt, even though they met with a failure which he regarded as inevitable.

His room was locked and in darkness, and he fumbled in his pocket for the key, found it, turned the lock, opened the door and entered.

“I wonder,” he mused, reaching out his hand and pressing down the switch of the light.

===Chinese===
主编一路爬楼梯，一路盘算这四位还能在他的报纸内容预告上霸占多久，倒巴不得他们当真动一动手——哪怕落一个他认定的必败结局。

他的房间锁着，一片漆黑；他在口袋里摸索钥匙，找着，转锁，开门，进屋。

「不知道啊。」他思忖着，伸手按下了电灯开关。

===Original===
There was a blinding flash, a quick splutter of flame, and the room was in darkness again.

Startled, he retreated to the corridor and called for a light.

“Send for the electrician,” he roared; “one of these damned fuses has gone!”

===Chinese===
一道白光眩目，火苗噼啪一窜，屋子重归黑暗。

他吓了一跳，退到走廊里，喊人掌灯来。

「去把电工找来！」他吼道，「准是哪根该死的保险丝烧了！」

===Original===
A lamp revealed the room to be filled with a pungent smoke; the electrician discovered that every globe had been carefully removed from its socket and placed on the table. From one of the brackets suspended a curly length of thin wire which ended in a small black box, and it was from this that thick fumes were issuing.

“Open the windows,” directed the editor; and a bucket of water having been brought, the little box was dropped carefully into it.

Then it was that the editor discovered the letter﻿—the greenish-grey letter that lay upon his desk. He took it up, turned it over, opened it, and noticed that the gum on the flap was still wet.

===Chinese===
灯来了，照见满屋辛辣的烟；电工发现，每只灯泡都被人细心地从灯座上旋下，摆在了桌上。一支灯架上垂下一段卷曲的细铁丝，丝端坠着一只小黑盒，浓烟正是从那盒子里涌出来的。

「把窗子都打开。」主编吩咐；一桶水端来，那只小盒被小心地浸了进去。

直到这时主编才发现那封信——端端正正躺在他书桌上的一封灰绿色信。他拾起来，翻了个身，拆开，注意到封口的胶还是湿的。

===Original===
> **Honoured Sir** (ran the note), when you turned on your light this evening you probably imagined for an instant that you were a victim of one of those “outrages” to which you are fond of referring. We owe you an apology for any annoyance we may have caused you. The removal of your lamp and the substitution of a “plug” connecting a small charge of magnesium powder is the cause of your discomfiture. We ask you to believe that it would have been as simple to have connected a charge of nitroglycerine, and thus have made you your own executioner. We have arranged this as evidence of our inflexible intention to carry out our promise in respect to the Alien Extradition Act. There is no power on earth that can save Sir Philip Ramon from destruction, and we ask you, as the directing force of a great medium, to throw your weight into the scale in the cause of justice, to call upon your Government to withdraw an unjust measure, and save, not only the lives of many inoffensive persons who have found an asylum in your country, but also the life of a Minister of the Crown whose only fault in our eyes is his zealousness in an unrighteous cause.

> (Signed) The Four Just Men

===Chinese===
> **敬启者**（信上写道）：今晚您开灯的一瞬，多半以为自己遭遇了您惯常挂在嘴边的那些「暴行」中的一次。若有搅扰，谨此致歉。您的灯泡被取下，换上一枚「插头」，接通了一小撮镁粉，这便是您方才狼狈的缘故。务请相信：换上一管硝化甘油，叫您亲手充当自己的行刑人，也一样的容易。我们布置这一切，是要向您证明：关于《外侨引渡法案》，我们兑现承诺的决心不可动摇。普天之下没有任何力量能救菲利普·拉蒙爵士免于毁灭。我们恳请您，作为一家大报的掌舵人，把您的分量压上正义的天平，吁请贵国政府撤回一项不义的法案——这保全的不只是贵国收留的众多无辜者的性命，还有一位王国大臣的性命；在我们眼中，他唯一的过错，不过是在一桩不义之业上过分热心。

> （签名）四义士

===Original===
“Whew!” whistled the editor, wiping his forehead and eyeing the soddened box floating serenely at the top of the bucket.

“Anything wrong, sir?” asked the electrician daringly.

“Nothing,” was the sharp reply. “Finish your work, refix these globes, and go.”

===Chinese===
「嚯！」主编吹了声口哨，抹着额头，盯住水桶里那只湿透的盒子悠悠地漂在水面。

「出什么事了吗，先生？」电工壮着胆子问。

「没什么。」回答很干脆。「干你的活，把灯泡装回去，走人。」

===Original===
The electrician, ill-satisfied and curious, looked at the floating box and the broken length of wire.

“Curious-looking thing, sir,” he said. “If you ask me﻿—”

“I don’t ask you anything; finish your work,” the great journalist interrupted.

“Beg pardon, I’m sure,” said the apologetic artisan.

===Chinese===
电工满腹狐疑又意难平，望着漂着的盒子和断掉的铁丝。

「这玩意儿模样可真怪，先生。」他说，「您要问我——」

「我什么也不问你；干你的活。」大记者打断了他。

「冒犯了，真的对不起。」匠人赔着不是。

===Original===
Half an hour later the editor of the *Megaphone* sat discussing the situation with Welby. Welby, who is the greatest foreign editor in London, grinned amiably and drawled his astonishment.

“I have always believed that these chaps meant business,” he said cheerfully, “and what is more, I feel pretty certain that they will keep their promise. When I was in Genoa”﻿—Welby got much of his information first hand﻿—“when I was in Genoa﻿—or was it Sofia?﻿—I met a man who told me about the Trelovitch affair. He was one of the men who assassinated the King of Serbia, you remember. Well, one night he left his quarters to visit a theatre﻿—the same night he was found dead in the public square with a sword thrust through his heart. There were two extraordinary things about it.” The foreign editor ticked them off on his fingers. “First, the general was a noted swordsman, and there was every evidence that he had not been killed in cold blood, but had been killed in a duel; the second was that he wore corsets, as many of these Germanized officers do, and one of his assailants had discovered this fact, probably by a sword thrust, and had made him discard them; at any rate when he was found this frippery was discovered close by his body.”

===Chinese===
半小时后，《传声筒报》主编坐下来，与韦尔比（Welby）合计眼下的局面。韦尔比是伦敦首屈一指的外国新闻主编，和气地咧嘴一笑，慢条斯理地说出他的惊讶。

「我向来相信这伙人来真的，」他兴致勃勃地说，「而且不妨说，我笃定他们会说到做到。我在热那亚的时候」——韦尔比的许多消息都是一手得来——「我在热那亚的时候——还是索菲亚来着？——碰到一个人，跟我讲过特雷洛维奇那桩事。他也是刺杀塞尔维亚国王的那伙人里的一个，记得吧。喏，一天夜里他离开住处去看戏——就在当晚，人们发现他死在广场上，一剑穿心。这事有两处邪门。」外国新闻主编扳着手指头数。「第一，这位将军是有名的剑客，种种迹象表明他不是被冷血杀害的，而是死于一场决斗；第二，他穿紧身褡——这些日耳曼化军官多有此好——袭击者之一发现了这个，多半是一剑试出来的，便逼他脱了；反正人们找到他时，这件脂粉行头就撂在尸体近旁。」

===Original===
“Was it known at the time that it was the work of the Four?” asked the editor.

Welby shook his head.

“Even I had never heard of them before,” he said resentfully. Then he asked, “What have you done about your little scare?”

“I’ve seen the hall porters and the messengers, and every man on duty at the time, but the coming and the going of our mysterious friend﻿—I don’t suppose there was more than one﻿—is unexplained. It really is a remarkable thing. Do you know, Welby, it gives me quite an uncanny feeling; the gum on the envelope was still wet; the letter must have been written on the premises and sealed down within a few seconds of my entering the room.”

===Chinese===
「当时就知道是四义士干的吗？」主编问。

韦尔比摇了摇头。

「连我都是头一回听说这名号。」他悻悻地说。随即问道：「您这场小小的惊魂，处置得怎么样了？」

「门房、信差、当时当班的每一个人，我都问过了，可咱们那位神秘朋友的来与去——我料想来的不过一人——终究没法解释。这真是咄咄怪事。你知道吗，韦尔比，我浑身直起邪毛；信封口的胶还是湿的；那封信必定是在楼里写的，在我进屋前几秒钟才封上。」

===Original===
“Were the windows open?”

“No; all three were shut and fastened, and it would have been impossible to enter the room that way.”

The detective who came to receive a report of the circumstances endorsed this opinion.

===Chinese===
「窗户开着吗？」

「没有；三扇都关得死死的，还上了闩，人休想从那里进屋。」

前来听取案情汇报的侦探对此说深表赞同。

===Original===
“The man who wrote this letter must have left your room not longer than a minute before you arrived,” he concluded, and took charge of the letter.

Being a young and enthusiastic detective, before finishing his investigations he made a most minute search of the room, turning up carpets, tapping walls, inspecting cupboards, and taking laborious and unnecessary measurements with a foot-rule.

“There are a lot of our chaps who sneer at detective stories,” he explained to the amused editor, “but I have read almost everything that has been written by Gaboriau and Conan Doyle, and I believe in taking notice of little things. There wasn’t any cigar ash or anything of that sort left behind, was there?” he asked, wistfully.

===Chinese===
「写这封信的人，至多在您到达前一分钟离开您的房间。」他下了结论，把信收走。

他是个年轻热切的侦探，调查收尾之前，把房间彻头彻尾搜了一遍：掀地毯，敲墙壁，查碗柜，还拿一柄折尺吃了力地量了些全无必要的尺寸。

「咱们同行里嗤笑侦探小说的不少，」他向忍着笑的主编解释道，「可加博里奥和柯南·道尔写的东西，我差不多读了个遍，我信奉小处留心。没留下雪茄灰之类的东西吧？」他满怀指望地问。

===Original===
“I’m afraid not,” said the editor, gravely.

“Pity,” said the detective, and wrapping up the “infernal machine” and its appurtenances, he took his departure.

Afterwards the editor informed Welby that the disciple of Holmes had spent half an hour with a magnifying glass examining the floor.

“He found half a sovereign that I lost weeks ago, so it’s really an ill wind﻿—”

===Chinese===
「恐怕没有。」主编正色道。

「可惜。」侦探说，把「爆炸装置」连同附件打了个包，告辞而去。

事后主编告诉韦尔比，那位福尔摩斯的门徒举着放大镜趴在地板上，足足察看了半个钟头。

「他找出了我几个星期前丢的半枚沙弗林，可见刮邪风也有邪福——」

===Original===
All that evening nobody but Welby and the chief knew what had happened in the editor’s rooms. There was some rumour in the subeditor’s department that a small accident had occurred in the sanctum.

“Chief busted a fuse in his room and got a devil of a fright,” said the man who attended to the shipping list.

“Dear me,” said the weather expert, looking up from his chart, “do you know something like that happened to me; the other night﻿—”

===Chinese===
那一晚，除了韦尔比和主编，没人知道主编房间里出了什么事。助理编辑部里隐约传着闲话，说圣所里出了点小事故。

「主编屋里烧了根保险丝，把魂都吓掉半条。」管航运版的那位说。

「哎呀，」气象专家从天气图上抬起头说，「你知不知道，我也遇上过类似的事；前几天夜里——」

===Original===
The chief had directed a few firm words to the detective before his departure.

“Only you and myself know anything about this occurrence,” said the editor, “so if it gets out I shall know it comes from Scotland Yard.”

“You may be sure nothing will come from us,” was the detective’s reply; “we’ve got into too much hot water already.”

“That’s good,” said the editor, and “that’s good” sounded like a threat.

===Chinese===
侦探临走前，主编对他说了几句斩钉截铁的话。

「这事只有你我二人知情，」主编说，「所以消息若是走了风，我便知道它是从苏格兰场走漏的。」

「放心，我们这边一个字也不会漏。」侦探答道，「我们惹的麻烦已经够多了。」

「那就好。」主编说，而这声「那就好」听来像一句威胁。

===Original===
So that Welby and the chief kept the matter a secret till half an hour before the paper went to press. This may seem to the layman an extraordinary circumstance, but experience has shown most men who control newspapers that news has an unlucky knack of leaking out before it appears in type. Wicked compositors﻿—and even compositors can be wicked﻿—have been known to screw up copies of important and exclusive news and to throw them out of a convenient window, where they have fallen close to a patient man standing in the street below and have been immediately hurried off to the office of a rival newspaper and sold for more than their weight in gold. Such cases have been known.

But at half-past eleven the buzzing hive of Megaphone House began to hum, for then it was that the subeditors learnt for the first time of the “outrage.”

===Chinese===
于是韦尔比和主编守口如瓶，一直守到开印前半小时。在外行人看来这或许不可思议，但大凡掌管过报纸的人都有经验：新闻这东西，不幸得很，天生一副在见报之前走漏的本事。歹毒的排字工——排字工也有歹毒的——干过的事可有过记载：把重要独家新闻的稿子揉成一团，从近便的窗口扔出去，恰好落在楼下街上一位有耐心的先生脚边，随即火速送往对头报馆，卖了个比金价还高的价钱。这类案子，确曾有过。

可到了十一点半，传声筒报大楼这座嗡嗡作响的蜂巢骚动起来了——正是在那时，助理编辑们头一回听说了这桩「暴行」。

===Original===
It was a great story﻿—still another *Megaphone* scoop, headlined half down the page with “The ‘Just Four’ again﻿—Outrage at the office of the *Megaphone*﻿—Devilish Ingenuity﻿—Another Threatening Letter﻿—The Four Will Keep Their Promise﻿—Remarkable Document﻿—Will the Police Save Sir Philip Ramon?”

“A very good story,” said the chief complacently, reading the proofs. He was preparing to leave, and was speaking to Welby by the door.

===Chinese===
这真是条大新闻——又是《传声筒报》一桩独得，通栏标题占去半版：「义士四人组再出手——传声筒报馆惊遭暴行——魔鬼般的巧思——又见恐吓信——四义士必践其诺——奇文共赏——警方救得了菲利普·拉蒙爵士吗？」

「好一条新闻。」主编读着清样，颇为自得。他正准备离开，在门边同韦尔比说话。

===Original===
“Not bad,” said the discriminating Welby. “What I think﻿—hullo!”

The last was addressed to a messenger who appeared with a stranger.

“Gentleman wants to speak to somebody, sir﻿—bit excited, so I brought him up; he’s a foreigner, and I can’t understand him, so I brought him to you”﻿—this to Welby.

“What do you want?” asked the chief in French.

===Chinese===
「不赖。」眼光挑剔的韦尔比说。「依我看——咦！」

后半句是对一名信差说的，他领着个陌生人来了。

「这位先生要找人说话，先生——有点激动，我就领他上来了；他是个外国人，我听不懂他的话，所以带给您」——这话是对韦尔比说的。

「你要什么？」主编用法语问。

===Original===
The man shook his head, and said a few words in a strange tongue.

“Ah!” said Welby. “Spanish﻿—what do you wish?” he said in that language.

“Is this the office of that paper?” The man produced a grimy copy of the *Megaphone*.

“Yes.”

===Chinese===
那人摇头，用一种陌生的语言说了几句。

「啊！」韦尔比说。「西班牙语——你有什么事？」他换上那种语言问。

「这儿就是那份报的报馆吗？」那人掏出一份油污的《传声筒报》。

「是。」

===Original===
“Can I speak to the editor?”

The chief looked suspicious.

“I am the editor,” he said.

The man looked over his shoulder, then leant forward.

“I am one of The Four Just Men,” he said hesitatingly.

===Chinese===
「我能跟主编说话吗？」

主编面露疑色。

「我就是主编。」他说。

那人回头看了看，随即探身向前。

「我……是四义士之一。」他吞吞吐吐地说。

===Original===
Welby took a step towards him and scrutinized him *closely*.

“What is your name,” he asked quickly.

“Miguel Thery of Jerez,” replied the man.

===Chinese===
韦尔比朝他跨近一步，把他从头到脚*仔仔细细*打量了一遍。

「你叫什么名字？」他疾声问。

「赫雷斯的米格尔·特里（Miguel Thery）。」那人答道。

---
===Original===
It was half-past ten when, returning from a concert, the cab that bore Poiccart and Manfred westward passed through Hanover Square and turned off to Oxford Street.

“You ask to see the editor,” Manfred was explaining; “they take you up to the offices﻿—you explain your business to somebody; they are very sorry, but they cannot help you; they are very polite, but not to the extent of seeing you off the premises, so, wandering about seeking your way out, you come to the editor’s room and, knowing that he is out, slip in, make your arrangements, walk out, locking the door after you if nobody is about, addressing a few farewell words to an imaginary occupant, if you are seen, and voilà!”

===Chinese===
十点半，听完音乐会归来的波伊卡特与曼弗雷德乘马车西行，穿过汉诺威广场（Hanover Square），拐上牛津街。

「你求见主编，」曼弗雷德正讲解着，「他们领你上楼到办公处——你把来意向某个人一说；他们万分抱歉，实在爱莫能助；他们客气极了，却又不至于把你送出门去，于是你四处转悠寻路，就转到了主编的房间，知道他不在，闪身进去，布置停当，再走出来：四下无人，便随手锁门；让人瞧见了，就冲屋里一位假想的住客道几句别——瞧，这不就成了！」

===Original===
Poiccart bit the end of his cigar.

“Use for your envelope a gum that will not dry under an hour and you heighten the mystery,” he said quietly, and Manfred was amused.

“The envelope-just-fastened is an irresistible attraction to an English detective.”

===Chinese===
波伊卡特咬了咬雪茄头。

「信封用一种一个钟头内干不了的胶，谜团便又深一层。」他淡淡地说，曼弗雷德听得有趣。

「『信才封上』这桩事，对英国侦探有一种无法抗拒的吸引力。」

===Original===
The cab speeding along Oxford Street turned into the Edgware Road, when Manfred put up his hand and pushed open the trap in the roof.

“We’ll get down here,” he called, and the driver pulled up to the sidewalk.

“I thought you said Pembridge Gardens?” he remarked, as Manfred paid him.

“So I did,” said Manfred; “good night.”

===Chinese===
马车沿牛津街疾驰，拐进埃奇韦尔路（Edgware Road），这时曼弗雷德抬手推开顶棚的活门。

「我们在这儿下。」他喊道，车夫把车靠向人行道。

「您说的不是潘布里奇花园（Pembridge Gardens）吗？」曼弗雷德付钱时，他咕哝了一句。

「正是。」曼弗雷德说，「晚安。」

===Original===
They waited chatting on the edge of the pavement until the cab had disappeared from view, then turned back to the Marble Arch, crossed to Park Lane, walked down that plutocratic thoroughfare and round into Piccadilly. Near the Circus they found a restaurant with a long bar and many small alcoves, where men sat round marble tables, drinking, smoking, and talking. In one of these, alone, sat Gonsalez, smoking a long cigarette and wearing on his clean-shaven mobile face a look of meditative content.

Neither of the men evinced the slightest sign of surprise at meeting him﻿—yet Manfred’s heart missed a beat, and into the pallid cheeks of Poiccart crept two bright red spots.

===Chinese===
两人在人行道边聊着等马车走远，看不见了，才折回大理石拱门（Marble Arch），穿过马路进公园巷（Park Lane），沿那条豪门大道走下去，拐进皮卡迪利（Piccadilly）。快到圆环时，他们寻见一家餐馆，长长的吧台，许多小雅座，男人们围坐在大理石桌旁，喝酒，抽烟，谈天。其中一座上，冈萨雷斯独自坐着，抽一支细长的烟，那张刮得干干净净、富于表情的脸上，带着一层沉思的满足。

两人见他，谁也没有半点惊讶的表示——然而曼弗雷德的心漏跳了一拍，波伊卡特苍白的腮上悄悄爬上两团鲜艳的红。

===Original===
They seated themselves, a waiter came and they gave their orders, and when he had gone Manfred asked in a low tone, “Where is Thery?”

Leon gave the slightest shrug.

“Thery has made his escape,” he answered, calmly.

===Chinese===
他们落了座，侍者过来，点了酒食，待人走后，曼弗雷德低声问：「特里呢？」

莱昂几不可察地耸了耸肩。

「特里跑了。」他平静地答道。

===Original===
For a minute neither man spoke, and Leon continued:

“This morning, before you left, you gave him a bundle of newspapers?”

Manfred nodded.

“They were English newspapers,” he said. “Thery does not know a word of English. There were pictures in them﻿—I gave them to amuse him.”

===Chinese===
两人有一分钟没说话，莱昂接着说：

「今天早上，你们出门前，是不是给了他一沓报纸？」

曼弗雷德点头。

「是英文报纸。」他说。「特里一个英文字也不识。里头有画——给他解闷的。」

===Original===
“You gave him, amongst others, the *Megaphone*?”

“Yes﻿—ha!” Manfred remembered.

“The offer of a reward was in it﻿—and the free pardon﻿—printed in Spanish.”

Manfred was gazing into vacancy.

===Chinese===
「其中就有《传声筒报》？」

「是——啊！」曼弗雷德想起来了。

「悬赏告示就在里面——还有特赦——是用西班牙文印的。」

曼弗雷德望着虚空出神。

===Original===
“I remember,” he said, slowly; “I read it afterwards.”

“It was very ingenious,” remarked Poiccart, commendingly.

“I noticed he was rather excited, but I accounted for this by the fact that we had told him last night of the method we intended adopting for the removal of Ramon and the part he was to play.”

Leon changed the topic to allow the waiter to serve the refreshments that had been ordered.

===Chinese===
「我记得。」他缓缓地说，「后来我读到了。」

「这一手实在高明。」波伊卡特称许道。

「我察觉他相当兴奋，可我把它归因于昨晚我们告诉了他除掉拉蒙的办法，以及他要演的角色。」

莱昂岔开话头，好让侍者上酒食。

===Original===
“It is preposterous,” he went on, without changing his key, “that a horse on which so much money has been placed should not have been sent to England at least a month in advance.”

“The idea of a bad Channel-crossing leading to the scratching of the favourite of a big race is unheard of,” added Manfred, severely.

The waiter left them.

“We went for a walk this afternoon,” resumed Leon, “and we were passing along Regent Street, he stopping every few seconds to look in the shops, when suddenly﻿—we had been staring at the window of a photographer’s﻿—I missed him. There were hundreds of people in the street﻿—but no Thery.﻿ ﻿… I have been seeking him ever since.”

===Chinese===
「真是岂有此理，」他接着说，声调不变，「一匹压了这么多钱的马，竟不提前至少一个月送来英国。」

「只因渡海峡受了点风浪，就把大赛的头马退出，闻所未闻。」曼弗雷德严厉地补充。

侍者退下了。

「今天下午我们出去散步，」莱昂续道，「我们正沿摄政街（Regent Street）走，他每隔几秒就停下来看橱窗，忽然——我们正盯着一家照相馆的橱窗——我一回头，人没了。街上有成百上千的人——却没有特里。……我从那时起一直找到现在。」

===Original===
Leon sipped his drink and looked at his watch.

The other two men did nothing, said nothing.

A careful observer might have noticed that both Manfred’s and Poiccart’s hands strayed to the top button of their coats.

“Perhaps not so bad as that,” smiled Gonsalez.

===Chinese===
莱昂呷着酒，看了看表。

另外两人不动，也不言语。

细心的看客兴许会留意到，曼弗雷德和波伊卡特两人的手，都悄悄摸上了大衣最上面那颗纽扣。

「也许没那么糟。」冈萨雷斯含笑道。

===Original===
Manfred broke the silence of the two.

“I take all blame,” he commenced, but Poiccart stopped him with a gesture.

“If there is any blame, I alone am blameless,” he said with a short laugh. “No, George, it is too late to talk of blame. We underrated the cunning of m’sieur, the enterprise of the English newspapers and﻿—and﻿—”

“The girl at Jerez,” concluded Leon.

===Chinese===
曼弗雷德打破了二人的沉默。

「责任全在我。」他刚开口，波伊卡特一抬手止住了他。

「真要论罪责，唯独我是清白的。」他短促地一笑。「不，乔治，如今再谈罪责已嫌太晚。我们低估了这位先生的狡猾，低估了英国报纸的进取，还有——还有——」

「赫雷斯的那姑娘。」莱昂替他说完。

===Original===
Five minutes passed in silence, each man thinking rapidly.

“I have a motorcar not far from here,” said Leon at length. “You had told me you would be at this place by eleven o’clock; we have the naphtha launch at Burnham-on-Crouch﻿—we could be in France by daybreak.”

Manfred looked at him. “What do you think yourself?” he asked.

“I say stay and finish the work,” said Leon.

===Chinese===
五分钟悄然过去，人人心里念头飞转。

「我有一辆汽车，就在离这儿不远的地方。」莱昂终于开口。「你们说过十一点会到这个地方；石脑油快艇就泊在伯纳姆（Burnham-on-Crouch）——天亮前我们就能到法国。」

曼弗雷德望着他。「你自己怎么想？」他问。

「我说，留下来，把事办完。」莱昂说。

===Original===
“And I,” said Poiccart quietly but decisively.

Manfred called the waiter.

“Have you the last editions of the evening papers?”

The waiter thought he could get them, and returned with two.

===Chinese===
「我也是。」波伊卡特平静而决绝地说。

曼弗雷德唤来侍者。

「有没有晚报的最后版？」

侍者说想想办法，回来时手里拿着两份。

===Original===
Manfred scanned the pages carefully, then threw them aside.

“Nothing in these,” he said. “If Thery has gone to the police we must hide and use some other method than that agreed upon, or we could strike now. After all, Thery has told us all we want to know, but﻿—”

“That would be unfair to Ramon.” Poiccart finished the sentence in such a tone as summarily ended that possibility. “He has still two days, and must receive yet another, and last, warning.”

“Then we must find Thery.”

===Chinese===
曼弗雷德细细翻了一遍，随手丢开。

「这些报上没有。」他说。「倘若特里去了警察那里，我们就得藏起来，改用约定之外的法子；要么，索性现在就动手。说到底，该让我们知道的，特里都告诉过我们，可是——」

「那对拉蒙不公平。」波伊卡特用一种把这念头一刀斩断的语气接完了这句话。「他还有两天，还该收到最后一道警告。」

「那就必须找到特里。」

===Original===
It was Manfred who spoke, and he rose, followed by Poiccart and Gonsalez.

“If Thery has not gone to the police﻿—where would he go?”

The tone of Leon’s question suggested the answer.

“To the office of the newspaper that published the Spanish advertisement,” was Manfred’s reply, and instinctively the three men knew that this was the correct solution.

“Your motorcar will be useful,” said Manfred, and all three left the bar.

===Chinese===
说话的是曼弗雷德，他站起身，波伊卡特和冈萨雷斯跟上。

「特里若不是去了警察那里——他会去哪儿？」

莱昂问话的调子已把答案带了出来。

「去登了西班牙文告示的那家报馆。」曼弗雷德答道，三人凭直觉都明白，这才是正解。

「你的汽车用得上了。」曼弗雷德说，三人离开了吧台。

---
===Original===
In the editor’s room Thery faced the two journalists.

“Thery?” repeated Welby. “I do not know that name. Where do you come from? What is your address?”

“I come from Jerez in Andalusia, from the wine farm of Sienor.”

“Not that,” interrupted Welby; “where do you come from now﻿—what part of London?”

===Chinese===
主编房间里，特里与两位记者对面而立。

「特里？」韦尔比重复了一遍。「没听过这名字。你从哪里来？住在哪里？」

「我从安达卢西亚的赫雷斯来，从谢诺尔（Sienor）的酒庄来。」

「不是问这个。」韦尔比打断道，「你现在从哪里来——伦敦哪一块？」

===Original===
Thery raised his hands despairingly.

“How should I know? There are houses and streets and people﻿—and it is in London, and I was to kill a man, a minister, because he had made a wicked law﻿—they did not tell me﻿—”

“They﻿—who?” asked the editor eagerly.

“The other three.”

===Chinese===
特里绝望地举起双手。

「我哪知道？到处是房子、街道和人——反正在伦敦；他们要我去杀一个人，一位大臣，因为他立了一条恶法——他们没告诉我——」

「他们——是谁？」主编急问。

「另外三个。」

===Original===
“But their names?”

Thery shot a suspicious glance at his questioner.

“There is a reward,” he said sullenly, “and a pardon. I want these before I tell﻿—”

The editor stepped to his desk.

===Chinese===
「叫什么名字？」

特里朝发问的人投去狐疑的一瞥。

「有悬赏，」他阴沉着脸说，「还有特赦。我要先拿到，再说——」

主编走到书桌前。

===Original===
“If you are one of the Four you shall have your reward﻿—you shall have some of it now.” He pressed a button and a messenger came to the door.

“Go to the composing-room and tell the printer not to allow his men to leave until I give orders.”

Below, in the basement, the machines were thundering as they flung out the first numbers of the morning news.

“Now”﻿—the editor turned to Thery, who had stood, uneasily shifting from foot to foot whilst the order was being given﻿—“now, tell me all you know.”

===Chinese===
「你若真是四义士之一，赏金少不了你的——现在就可以先给你一些。」他按了铃，一名信差来到门口。

「去排字房，告诉领班，我不下令，不许放他的人走。」

楼下地下室里，机器轰鸣着，把早晨新闻的最初几份吐了出来。

「好了」——主编转向特里；方才下令时，那人一直站着，两脚不安地倒换——「好了，把你所知道的统统告诉我。」

===Original===
Thery did not answer; his eyes were fixed on the floor.

“There is a reward and a pardon,” he muttered doggedly.

“Hasten!” cried Welby. “You will receive your reward and the pardon also. Tell us, who are the Four Just Men; who are the other three; where are they to be found?”

“Here,” said a clear voice behind him; and he turned as a stranger, closing the door as he entered, stood facing the three men﻿—a stranger in evening dress, masked from brow to chin.

===Chinese===
特里不答；他的眼睛钉在地板上。

「有悬赏，还有特赦。」他犟着头咕哝。

「快说！」韦尔比喊道。「赏金和特赦都归你。告诉我们，四义士是哪些人；另外三个是谁；到哪里找他们？」

「在这儿。」他身后响起一个清朗的声音；他转过身，只见一个陌生人进门便掩上了门，正与三人对面而立——晚礼服装束，从额头到下巴蒙着面。

===Original===
There was a revolver in the hand that hung at his side.

“I am one,” repeated the stranger calmly; “there are two others waiting outside the building.”

“How did you get here﻿—what do you want?” demanded the editor, and stretched his hand to an open drawer in his desk.

“Take your hand away”﻿—and the thin barrel of the revolver rose with a jerk. “How I came here your doorkeeper will explain, when he recovers consciousness. Why I am here is because I wish to save my life﻿—not an unreasonable wish. If Thery speaks I may be a dead man﻿—I am about to prevent his speaking. I have no quarrel with either of you gentlemen, but if you hinder me I shall kill you,” he said, simply. He spoke all the while in English, and Thery, with wide-stretched eyes and distended nostrils, shrank back against the wall, breathing quickly.

===Chinese===
他垂在身侧的手里握着一支左轮枪。

「我就是其一。」陌生人不动声色地重说了一遍；「还有两位，在楼外候着。」

「你怎么进来的——想干什么？」主编厉声质问，手朝书桌上一只敞开的抽屉伸去。

「把手拿开」——左轮枪细长的枪管猛地一抬。「我怎么进来的，等你们门房醒过来，自会说明。我到这儿来，是因为想保住我这条命——这愿望不算过分。特里一开口，我兴许就是死人——我这就来堵他的嘴。我与两位先生无冤无仇，但你们若拦我，我就杀你们。」他说得平平淡淡。他自始至终说的是英语；特里双眼圆睁，鼻翼翕张，缩到墙根，呼吸急促。

===Original===
“You,” said the masked man, turning to the terror-stricken informer and speaking in Spanish, “would have betrayed your comrades﻿—you would have thwarted a great purpose, therefore it is just that you should die.”

He raised the revolver to the level of Thery’s breast, and Thery fell on his knees, mouthing the prayer he could not articulate.

“By God﻿—no!” cried the editor, and sprang forward.

The revolver turned on him.

===Chinese===
「你，」蒙面人转向那吓破胆的告密者，换用西班牙语说，「本要出卖你的同伴——本要坏了大事，因此，你死得不冤。」

他把左轮枪抬到特里胸口的高度，特里双膝一软跪了下去，嘴动着，祷词却一个字也吐不出来。

「上帝在上——不许！」主编喊了一声，纵身扑上。

左轮枪转过来对准了他。

===Original===
“Sir,” said the unknown﻿—and his voice sunk almost to a whisper﻿—“for God’s sake do not force me to kill you.”

“You shall not commit a cold-blooded murder,” cried the editor in a white heat of anger, and moved forward, but Welby held him back.

“What is the use?” said Welby, in an undertone. “He means it﻿—we can do nothing.”

“You can do something,” said the stranger, and his revolver dropped to his side.

===Chinese===
「先生，」那陌生人说——嗓音低得几乎成了耳语——「看在上帝份上，别逼我杀你。」

「你不能就这么冷血杀人。」主编盛怒之下又抢步上前，却被韦尔比死死拉住。

「有什么用？」韦尔比低声道。「他动真格的——我们无能为力。」

「有一样你们做得到。」陌生人说，左轮枪垂回身侧。

===Original===
Before the editor could answer there was a knock at the door.

“Say you are busy,” and the revolver covered Thery, who was a whimpering, huddled heap by the wall.

“Go away,” shouted the editor, “I am busy.”

“The printers are waiting,” said the voice of the messenger.

===Chinese===
主编还没来得及答话，门上响起敲击。

「就说你正忙着。」左轮枪又罩住特里——他已是墙根下一团呜咽的烂泥。

「走开。」主编扬声道，「我忙着。」

「排字工还等着呢。」门外传来信差的声音。

===Original===
“Now,” asked the chief, as the footsteps of the boy died away, “what can we do?”

“You can save this man’s life.”

“How?”

“Give me your word of honour that you will allow us both to depart, and will neither raise an alarm nor leave this room for a quarter of an hour.”

===Chinese===
「好了。」男孩的脚步声远去后，主编问道，「我们能做什么？」

「你们能救下这个人一条命。」

「怎么救？」

「以你们的名誉向我担保：放我们两人离开，而且一刻钟之内，既不声张，也不出这间屋子。」

===Original===
The editor hesitated.

“How do I know that the murder you contemplate will not be committed as soon as you get clear?”

The other laughed under his mask.

“How do I know that as soon as I have left the room you will not raise an alarm?”

===Chinese===
主编犹豫了。

「我怎么知道，等你们一脱身，你蓄谋的谋杀不会立刻就办？」

对方在面具后笑了。

「我又怎么知道，我一出这屋子，你们不会立刻报警？」

===Original===
“I should have given my word, sir,” said the editor, stiffly.

“And I mine,” was the quiet response, “and my word has never been broken.”

In the editor’s mind a struggle was going on; here in his hand was the greatest story of the century; another minute and he would have extracted from Thery the secret of the Four.

Even now a bold dash might save everything﻿—and the printers were waiting﻿ ﻿… but the hand that held the revolver was the hand of a resolute man, and the chief yielded.

===Chinese===
「我本要给出我的担保，先生。」主编硬邦邦地说。

「我也本要给出我的。」回答安安静静，「而我的话，从不落空。」

主编心里正打着仗：世纪大新闻就在他手边；再有一分钟，他就能从特里嘴里挖出四义士的秘密。

即便此刻，孤注一掷或许还能保全一切——排字工还在等着……可握着左轮枪的那只手，是一只说一不二的手，主编让步了。

===Original===
“I agree, but under protest,” he said, “I warn you that your arrest and punishment is inevitable.”

“I regret,” said the masked man with a slight bow, “that I cannot agree with you﻿—nothing is inevitable save death. Come, Thery,” he said, speaking in Spanish. “On my word as a caballero, I will not harm you.”

Thery hesitated, then slunk forward with his head bowed and his eyes fixed on the floor.

===Chinese===
「我答应，但保留异议。」他说，「我警告你，你的落网与受刑，势不可免。」

「很遗憾，」蒙面人微微欠身，「这一点恕难苟同——万事皆非必然，唯有死除外。走吧，特里。」他换用西班牙语说。「以一个西班牙绅士（caballero）的名分起誓，我不伤你。」

特里迟疑了一下，随即低着头、眼盯地板，怯怯挪上前去。

===Original===
The masked man opened the door an inch, listened, and in the moment came the inspiration of the editor’s life.

“Look here,” he said quickly, the man giving place to the journalist, “when you get home will you write us an article about yourselves? You needn’t give us any embarrassing particulars, you know﻿—something about your aspirations, your raison d’être.”

“Sir,” said the masked man﻿—and there was a note of admiration in his voice﻿—“I recognize in you an artist. The article will be delivered tomorrow,” and opening the door the two men stepped into the darkened corridor.

===Chinese===
蒙面人把门开了一线，侧耳听了听——就在这一瞬，主编此生最大的一次灵光降临了。

「我说，」他疾声道——记者压倒了那人——「回到住处，给我们写一篇你们自己的文章如何？你知道，不必给什么叫人为难的细节——谈谈你们的抱负，你们的存在理由（raison d'être），就行。」

「先生，」蒙面人说——嗓音里带着一丝叹服——「我认出你是一位艺术家。文章明日奉上。」门开了，两人跨进幽暗的走廊。

## VII The Clues / 七 线索
===Original===
Blood-red placards, hoarse newsboys, overwhelming headlines, and column after column of leaded type told the world next day how near the Four had been to capture. Men in the train leaned forward, their newspapers on their knees, and explained what they would have done had they been in the editor of the *Megaphone*’s position. People stopped talking about wars and famines and droughts and street accidents and parliaments and ordinary everyday murders in order to concentrate their minds upon the topic of the hour. Would the Four Just Men carry out their promise and slay the secretary for foreign affairs on the morrow? Nothing else was spoken about. Here was a murder threatened a month ago, and, unless something unforeseen happened, to be committed tomorrow. No wonder that the London press devoted the greater part of its space to discuss the coming of Thery and his recapture.

“… It is not so easy to understand,” said the *Telegram*, “why, having the miscreants in their hands, certain journalists connected with a sensational and halfpenny contemporary allowed them to go free to work their evil designs upon a great statesman whose unparalleled.﻿ ﻿… We say ‘if,’ for, unfortunately, in these days of cheap journalism every story emanating from the sanctum sanctorum of sensation-loving sheets is not to be accepted on its pretensions; so if, as it is stated, these desperadoes really did visit the office of a contemporary last night﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
次日，血红的招贴、喊哑嗓子的报童、遮天蔽日的大标题、一栏接一栏的铅字，向世人宣告四义士离落网曾有何等之近。火车上的男人们俯身向前，报纸摊在膝头，讲说着假如自己处在《传声筒报》主编的位置会如何处置。人们不再谈战争、饥荒、旱情、车祸、议会和寻常巷陌的凶杀，好把心思集中在眼下这个题目上：四义士会不会履行诺言，明日便取外交大臣性命？再没有别的可谈。这是一桩一个月前就预告了的谋杀，除非天有不测，明天就要付诸实行。无怪乎伦敦各报把大半版面用来议论特里的到来与他的再度落网。

「……实在叫人难以理解，」《电讯报》（*Telegram*）说，「某些与一家耸人听闻的便士同行有关联的记者，既然恶徒已在掌中，何以竟放任其自由离去，任其向一位伟大政治家实施邪恶图谋，而此公之无出其右的……我们之所以说『如果』，是因为不幸，在这个新闻廉价的年代，凡出自好耸动的报纸之至圣所的每一个故事，都不能照它自命的账面价值全盘接受；所以，倘若果如其言，这伙亡命之徒昨夜当真造访了某同行报馆……」

===Original===
At noonday Scotland Yard circulated broadcast a hastily printed sheet:

===Chinese===
正午时分，苏格兰场向四方散发了一张匆促印就的传单：

===Original===
> **Wanted**, on suspicion of being connected with a criminal organization known as the Four Just Men, **Miguel Thery**, alias **Saimont**, alias **Le Chico**, late of Jerez, Spain, a Spaniard speaking no English. Height five feet eight inches. Eyes brown, hair black, slight black moustache, face broad. Scars: white scar on cheek, old knife wound on body. Figure, thickset.

> The above reward will be paid to any person or persons who shall give such information as shall lead to the identification of the said Thery with the band known as the Four Just Men, and his apprehension.

===Chinese===
> **通缉**：涉嫌与号称「四义士」之犯罪组织有涉，**米格尔·特里（Miguel Thery）**，化名**赛蒙特（Saimont）**，化名**勒奇科（Le Chico）**，原籍西班牙赫雷斯，西班牙人，不谙英语。身高五英尺八英寸。眼棕色，发黑，蓄稀疏黑色髭，阔面。疤痕：颊上有白疤，身上有旧刀伤。体格壮实。

> 凡有任何人士提供情报，足使上述特里被确认为「四义士」团伙成员并落网者，即以前述赏金酬谢。

===Original===
From which may be gathered that, acting on the information furnished by the editor and his assistant at two o’clock in the morning, the direct Spanish cable had been kept busy; important personages had been roused from their beds in Madrid, and the history of Thery as recorded in the Bureau had been reconstructed from pigeonhole records for the enlightenment of an energetic commissioner of police.

Sir Philip Ramon, sitting writing in his study at Portland Place, found a difficulty in keeping his mind upon the letter that lay before him. It was a letter addressed to his agent at Branfell, the huge estate over which he, in the years when he was out of office, played squire. Neither wife nor chick nor child had Sir Philip. “… if by any chance these men succeed in carrying out their purpose I have made ample provision not only for yourself but for all who have rendered me faithful service,” he wrote﻿—from which may be gathered the tenor of his letter.

===Chinese===
由此可见，凭着主编及其助手凌晨两点提供的情报，直通西班牙的海底电缆忙了个不亦乐乎；马德里的要人们被从床上唤起，档案局格子间里的记录被重新拼合成特里的履历，供一位干劲十足的警察总监参详。

菲利普·拉蒙爵士坐在波特兰街的书房里写信，却难以把心思收在眼前这封上。信是写给他在布兰费尔（Branfell）的地产经纪人的——他赋闲在野的那几年，就在那片广袤的庄园上做乡绅。菲利普爵士无妻无子，也无雏。「……倘若这些人万一得遂其愿，我已备下充裕的安排，不独为你，也为所有忠心侍奉过我的人。」他如此写道——信的基调，由此可知。

===Original===
During these past few weeks, Sir Philip’s feelings towards the possible outcome of his action had undergone a change.

The irritation of a constant espionage, friendly on the one hand, menacing on the other, had engendered so bitter a feeling of resentment, that in this newer emotion all personal fear had been swallowed up. His mind was filled with one unswerving determination, to carry through the measure he had in hand, to thwart the Four Just Men, and to vindicate the integrity of a Minister of the Crown. “It would be absurd,” he wrote in the course of an article entitled “Individuality in its Relation to the Public Service,” which was published some months later in the *Quarterly Review*﻿—“it would be monstrous to suppose that incidental criticism from a wholly unauthoritative source should affect or in any way influence a member of the Government in his conception of the legislation necessary for the millions of people entrusted to his care. He is the instrument, duly appointed, to put into tangible form the wishes and desires of those who naturally look to him to furnish, not only means and methods for the betterment of their conditions, or the amelioration of irksome restrictions upon international commercial relations, but to find them protection from risks extraneous of purely commercial liabilities﻿ ﻿… in such a case a Minister of the Crown with a due appreciation of his responsibilities ceases to exist as a man and becomes merely an unhuman automaton.”

===Chinese===
这几个星期里，菲利普爵士对自己行动可能招致的下场，心态已然变了。

无时不在的监视——一边是好意，一边是杀机——激起的怨愤如此酷烈，以至于这股新情绪把一切对自身的恐惧都吞没了。他心里只剩一桩不动摇的决心：把手头的措施办到底，挫败四义士，捍卫一位王国大臣的刚正。「若以为来自一个全无权威可言的来源的偶发批评，竟会影响政府成员对立法的构想——那立法关乎托付于他照管的千百万民众——那未免荒谬，」他在一篇文章里写道，该文题为《个性与公职之关系》，数月后刊于《评论季刊》（*Quarterly Review*）——「那简直是岂有此理。他是受正式任命的工具，要把那些人的愿望与欲求化为有形；那些人自然而然地指望他供给的，不仅是改善他们境遇的手段方法，或疏解国际贸易往来上恼人的限制，还要他替他们防避纯粹商债之外的种种风险……在这种情形下，一位对自己的责任有充分体认的王国大臣，便不复作为一个人存在，而只是一具没有人的气息的自动机器。」

===Original===
Sir Philip Ramon was a man with very few friends. He had none of the qualities that go to the making of a popular man. He was an honest man, a conscientious man, a strong man. He was the cold-blooded, cynical creature that a life devoid of love had left him. He had no enthusiasm﻿—and inspired none. Satisfied that a certain procedure was less wrong than any other, he adopted it. Satisfied that a measure was for the immediate or ultimate good of his fellows, he carried that measure through to the bitter end. It may be said of him that he had no ambitions﻿—only aims. He was the dangerous man in the cabinet, which he dominated in his masterful way, for he knew not the meaning of the blessed word “compromise.”

If he held views on any subject under the sun, those views were to be the views of his colleagues.

===Chinese===
菲利普·拉蒙爵士朋友寥寥。他身上没有一样讨人喜欢的资质。他诚实，尽责，坚强。他是被无爱的一生剩下的那件东西：一个冷血、玩世的人。他无所谓热忱——也激发不起别人的热忱。一旦认定某一做法比别的少些错处，他便采用它。一旦认定某一措施于同胞有眼前或最终的益处，他便把这措施办到山穷水尽。可以说他没有什么野心——只有目标。他是内阁里那个危险的人物，以他专断的做派驾驭着整个内阁，因为他不识「妥协」这个福词的滋味。

只要他对天下任何一桩事情有了看法，这看法就得成为同僚们的看法。

===Original===
Four times in the short history of the administration had “Rumoured Resignation of a Cabinet Minister” filled the placards of the newspapers, and each time the minister whose resignation was ultimately recorded was the man whose views had clashed with the foreign secretary. In small things, as in great, he had his way.

His official residence he absolutely refused to occupy, and No. 44, Downing Street was converted into half office, half palace. Portland Place was his home, and thence he drove every morning, passing the Horse Guards clock as it finished the last stroke of ten.

===Chinese===
本届政府短短的历史上，「内阁大臣传闻将辞」的标题已四度占满报端，而每一次，最终记录在案的辞职者，都是那位看法与外交大臣相左的人。小事如此，大事也如此，他无往不得逞。

官邸他坚决不住，唐宁街 44 号于是改成了半办公室、半宫殿。波特兰街才是他的家，他每天早晨从那里驱车出发，经过禁卫骑兵队的钟楼时，时钟恰敲完十点的最后一响。

===Original===
A private telephone wire connected his study in Portland Place with the official residence, and with the exception of this Sir Philip had cut himself adrift from the house in Downing Street, to occupy which had been the ambition of the great men of his party. Now, however, with the approach of the day on which their every effort would be taxed, the police insisted upon his taking up his quarters in Downing Street. Here, they said, the task of protecting the minister would be simplified. No. 44, Downing Street they knew. The approaches could be better guarded, and moreover the drive﻿—that dangerous drive!﻿—between Portland Place and the Foreign Office would be obviated.

It took a considerable amount of pressure and pleading to induce Sir Philip to take even this step, and it was only when it was pointed out that the surveillance to which he was being subjected would not be so apparent to himself that he yielded.

===Chinese===
一条专线把他在波特兰街的书房与官邸连着，除此之外，菲利普爵士同唐宁街那座房子一刀两断——住进那座房子，本是他党内大人物的夙愿。然而如今，那个要让警方倾尽全力的日子临近了，警察们执意要他搬进唐宁街。他们说，在那儿，保护大臣的差事可以省简些。唐宁街 44 号他们知根知底。各条进路更易把守，再说，波特兰街与外交部之间那段车程——那要命的车程！——也可以免了。

好说歹说，费了九牛二虎之力，才劝动菲利普爵士走出这一步；直到有人点破：在唐宁街，他受的监视不至于这样碍他自己的眼，他才让步。

===Original===
“You don’t like to find my men outside your door with your shaving-water,” said Superintendent Falmouth bluntly. “You objected to one of my men being in your bathroom when you went in the other morning, and you complained about a plainclothes officer driving on your box﻿—well, Sir Philip, in Downing Street I promise that you shan’t even see them.”

This clinched the argument.

===Chinese===
「您不爱一开门，送剃须热水的旁边就站着我的人。」法尔茅斯警司直统统地说。「那天早上您进浴室，里头有我一个人，您嫌了；便衣警官坐上您的车夫座，您也抱怨——好啦，菲利普爵士，到了唐宁街，我担保您连他们的影子都见不着。」

这一句把事情敲定了。

===Original===
It was just before leaving Portland Place to take up his new quarters that he sat writing to his agent whilst the detective waited outside the door. The telephone at Sir Philip’s elbow buzzed﻿—he hated bells﻿—and the voice of his private secretary asked with some anxiety how long he would be.

“We have got sixty men on duty at 44,” said the secretary, zealous and young, “and today and tomorrow we shall﻿—” and Sir Philip listened with growing impatience to the recital.

“I wonder you have not got an iron safe to lock me in,” he said petulantly, and closed the conversation.

===Chinese===
就在离开波特兰街搬往新居之前，他坐在那里给经纪人写信，侦探在门外候着。菲利普爵士手边的电话嗡嗡响了——他最恨铃——私人秘书的声音不无焦灼地问他还要多久。

「我们在 44 号布置了六十个人当值，」年轻而热心的秘书说，「今明两天我们还要——」菲利普爵士听着这一篇流水账，不耐烦与日俱增。

「怎么不干脆弄一座铁保险柜，把我锁进去。」他没好气地说，挂断了通话。

===Original===
There was a knock at the door and Falmouth put his head inside.

“I don’t want to hurry you, sir,” he said, “but﻿—”

So the foreign secretary drove off to Downing Street in something remarkably like a temper. For he was not used to being hurried, or taken charge of, or ordered hither and thither. It irritated him further to see the now familiar cyclists on either side of the carriage, to recognize at every few yards an obvious policeman in plain clothes admiring the view from the sidewalk, and when he came to Downing Street and found it barred to all carriages but his own, and an enormous crowd of morbid sightseers gathered to cheer his ingress, he felt as he had never felt before in his life﻿—humiliated.

===Chinese===
门上起了敲击声，法尔茅斯把头探进来。

「不想催您，先生，」他说，「可是——」

外交大臣就这样驱车前往唐宁街，那架势分明是一肚皮脾气。他不惯被人催，被人管，被人呼来喝去。看见马车两侧如今已看熟了的自行车手，每隔几码就认出一个站在人行道上赏风景的分明是便衣警察，已经够叫他恼火；待到了唐宁街，发现除他自己的车外一切车辆不得通行，一大群猎奇看客聚着为他的进场欢呼，他生平常一遭尝到了那种滋味——屈辱。

===Original===
He found his secretary waiting in his private office with the rough draft of the speech that was to introduce the second reading of the Extradition Bill.

“We are pretty sure to meet with a great deal of opposition,” informed the secretary, “but Mainland has sent out three-line whips, and expects to get a majority of thirty-six﻿—at the very least.”

===Chinese===
私人办公室里，秘书正等着他，手里拿着为引渡法案二读准备的演讲草稿。

「咱们多半会遇上强有力的抵制，」秘书禀报道，「不过梅因兰（Mainland）已发出了最紧急的党鞭令，料定能拿到三十六票的多数——往少了说。」

===Original===
Ramon read over the notes and found them refreshing. They brought back the old feeling of security and importance. After all, he was a great minister of state. Of course the threats were too absurd﻿—the police were to blame for making so much fuss; and of course the press﻿—yes, that was it﻿—a newspaper sensation. There was something buoyant, something almost genial in his air, when he turned with a half smile to his secretary.

“Well, what about my unknown friends﻿—what do the blackguards call themselves?﻿—the Four Just Men?” Even as he spoke he was acting a part; he had not forgotten their title, it was with him day and night.

===Chinese===
拉蒙把讲稿过了一遍，只觉神清气爽。那旧日安稳与分量的感觉回来了。说到底，他是堂堂国务大臣。那些威胁自然荒唐透顶——都怪警察小题大做；至于报界——对，就是这么回事——一场报馆制造的轰动。他半含笑意转向秘书时，神情里有一种轻快，一种近乎温和的东西。

「那么，我那些素未谋面的朋友们怎么样了——那伙恶棍管自己叫什么来着？——四义士？」话虽这样说，他演的却是戏；他们的名号他何曾忘过，日日夜夜都跟着他。

===Original===
The secretary hesitated; between his chief and himself the Four Just Men had been a tabooed subject.

“They﻿—oh, we’ve heard nothing more than you have read,” he said lamely; “we know now who Thery is, but we can’t place his three companions.”

The Minister pursed his lips.

===Chinese===
秘书迟疑了；在长官与他之间，四义士一向是个忌讳话题。

「他们——哦，我们听到的，不比您读到的多。」他讷讷地说；「特里是谁，现在知道了，可他那三个同伴，我们还对不上号。」

大臣抿了抿嘴唇。

===Original===
“They give me till tomorrow night to recant,” he said.

“You have heard from them again?”

“The briefest of notes,” said Sir Philip lightly.

“And otherwise?”

Sir Philip frowned. “They will keep their promise,” he said shortly, for the “otherwise” of his secretary had sent a coldness into his heart that he could not quite understand.

===Chinese===
「他们限我到明晚悔改。」他说。

「他们又来信了？」

「再简短不过的一封。」菲利普爵士轻描淡写地说。

「不然呢？」

菲利普爵士皱眉。「他们会履行诺言的。」他简短地说——秘书这一声「不然呢」，往他心里送进一股寒意，他自己也说不清来路。

---
===Original===
In the top room in the workshop at Carnaby Street, Thery, subdued, sullen, fearful, sat facing the three.

“I want you to understand,” said Manfred, “that we bear you no ill-will for what you have done. I think, and Señor Poiccart thinks, that Señor Gonsalez did right to spare your life and bring you back to us.”

===Chinese===
卡纳比街工场的顶层小屋里，特里颓唐、阴郁、惶恐，坐在三人对面。

「要你明白，」曼弗雷德说，「你干下的事，我们并不记恨。我，还有波伊卡特先生，都认为冈萨雷斯先生留你性命、把你带回我们身边，做得对。」

===Original===
Thery dropped his eyes before the half-quizzical smile of the speaker.

“Tomorrow night you will do as you agreed to do﻿—if the necessity still exists. Then you will go﻿—” he paused.

“Where?” demanded Thery in sudden rage. “Where, in the name of Heaven? I have told them my name, they will know who I am﻿—they will find that by writing to the police. Where am I to go?” He sprang to his feet, glowering on the three men, his hands trembling with rage, his great frame shaking with the intensity of his anger.

===Chinese===
说话人半带揶揄的微笑下，特里垂下了眼睛。

「明晚，你依约行事——倘若还有此必要。然后你就走——」他顿住了。

「去哪儿？」特里勃然大怒。「老天在上，去哪儿？我把名字告诉他们了，他们会知道我是谁——写封信给警察就查出来了。我能去哪儿？」他跳起来，怒视三人，双手气得发抖，高大的身架因盛怒而颤动。

===Original===
“You betrayed yourself,” said Manfred quietly; “that is your punishment. But we will find a place for you, a new Spain under other skies﻿—and the girl at Jerez shall be there waiting for you.”

Thery looked from one to the other suspiciously. Were they laughing at him? There was no smile on their faces; Gonsalez alone looked at him with keen, inquisitive eyes, as though he saw some hidden meaning in the speech.

“Will you swear that?” asked Thery hoarsely, “will you swear that by the﻿—”

===Chinese===
「是你自己出卖了自己。」曼弗雷德平静地说；「这便是你的惩罚。但我们会给你找个去处，另一方天空下的新西班牙——赫雷斯的那姑娘，会在那儿等你。」

特里狐疑地把三人挨个看过去。他们在耍笑他吗？谁脸上都没有笑；唯有冈萨雷斯用锐利而探究的目光望着他，仿佛从话里看出了什么深藏的意味。

「你们肯起誓吗？」特里哑着嗓子问，「肯凭着——起誓吗？」

===Original===
“I promise that﻿—if you wish it, I will swear it,” said Manfred. “And now,” he went on, his voice changing, “you know what is expected of you tomorrow night﻿—what you have to do?”

Thery nodded.

“There must be no hitch﻿—no bungling; you and I and Poiccart and Gonsalez will kill this unjust man in a way that the world will never guess﻿—such an execution as shall appal mankind﻿—a swift death, a sure death, a death that will creep through cracks, that will pass by the guards unnoticed. Why, there never has been such a thing done﻿—such﻿—” he stopped dead, with flushed cheeks and kindling eyes, and met the gaze of his two companions, Poiccart, impassive, sphinxlike, Leon, interested and analytic. Manfred’s face went a duller red. “I am sorry,” he said almost humbly; “for the moment I had forgotten the cause, and the end, in the strangeness of the means.” He raised his hand deprecatingly.

===Chinese===
「我应许——你若要，我便起誓。」曼弗雷德说。「现在，」他接着说，声调一变，「明晚要你做的，你都知道了——该干什么，明白吗？」

特里点头。

「不许有一丝差错——不许有半点笨拙；你我，加上波伊卡特和冈萨雷斯，要用一个世人永远猜不透的法子杀死这不义之人——一场将令全人类胆寒的行刑——迅疾的死，确凿的死，能从缝隙里爬进去的死，能从卫兵眼皮底下穿过去而不被察觉的死。嘿，这样的事还从来没有过——这样——」他戛然而止，双颊涨红，目光灼灼，迎上两位同伴的注视：波伊卡特无动于衷，斯芬克斯一般；莱昂兴致勃勃，条分缕析。曼弗雷德的脸红得更深了。「抱歉，」他说得近乎谦卑，「一时之间，手段之奇让我忘了初衷，也忘了结局。」他抬起手，止住了话头。

===Original===
“It is understandable,” said Poiccart gravely, and Leon pressed Manfred’s arm.

The three stood in embarrassed silence for a moment, then Manfred laughed.

“To work!” he said, and led the way to the improvised laboratory.

===Chinese===
「可以理解。」波伊卡特郑重地说，莱昂按了按曼弗雷德的手臂。

三人尴尬地静立了片刻，随后曼弗雷德笑了。

「干活！」他说，领头向那间临时实验室走去。

===Original===
Inside, Thery took off his coat. Here was his province, and from being the cowed dependent he took charge of the party, directing them, instructing, commanding, until he had the men of whom, a few minutes before, he had stood in terror, running from studio to laboratory, from floor to floor. There was much to be done, much testing, much calculating, many little sums to be worked out on paper, for in the killing of Sir Philip Ramon all the resources of modern science were to be pressed into the service of the Four.

“I am going to survey the land,” said Manfred suddenly, and disappearing into the studio returned with a pair of stepladders. These he straddled in the dark passage, and mounting quickly pushed up a trapdoor that led to the flat roof of the building. He pulled himself up carefully, crawled along the leaden surface, and raising himself cautiously looked over the low parapet. He was in the centre of a half-mile circle of uneven roofs. Beyond the circumference of his horizon London loomed murkily through smoke and mist. Below was the busy street. He took a hasty survey of the roof with its chimney stacks, its unornamental telegraph pole, its leaden floor and rusty guttering; then, through a pair of field-glasses, he made a long careful survey southward. He crawled slowly back to the trapdoor, raised it, and let himself down very gingerly till his feet touched the top of the ladder. Then he descended rapidly, closing the door after him.

===Chinese===
进了实验室，特里脱下外衣。这里是他的领地；方才还是受气的附庸，转眼便接管了全队，调遣他们，指点，发号施令，不多时，已把几分钟前他还战栗畏服的那几个人，支使得从摄影棚跑向实验室，从一层楼奔向另一层楼。要做的很多，要试验的很多，要计算的很多，许多小算式要落到纸面——因为在杀死菲利普·拉蒙爵士这件事上，现代科学的全部家当，都要征来为四义士效力。

「我去踏勘一下地形。」曼弗雷德突然说，闪进摄影棚，回来时扛着一副折梯。他把折梯在黑暗的过道里跨稳，三两下爬上去，顶开了通往平屋顶的活板门。他小心地引体上去，沿着铅皮屋面爬行，谨慎地直起身，从矮女儿墙上方望出去。他正处在一个半英里方圆的参差屋顶的圆心。视界的边缘之外，伦敦在烟雾里黑沉沉地隆起。脚下是忙碌的街道。他匆匆看了一遍屋顶——烟囱丛、无饰的电报线杆、铅皮地面、锈蚀的檐沟；随后举起一副望远镜，朝南做了长久而仔细的观察。他慢慢爬回活板门，掀开，小心翼翼放下身去，直到双脚够着梯顶，才迅速落下来，随手掩上活门。

===Original===
“Well?” asked Thery, with something of triumph in his voice.

“I see you have labelled it,” said Manfred.

“It is better so﻿—since we shall work in the dark,” said Thery.

===Chinese===
「怎么样？」特里问，话音里带着几分得意。

「看见你做的记号了。」曼弗雷德说。

「这样更好——反正我们要摸黑干活。」特里说。

===Original===
“Did you see then﻿—?” began Poiccart.

Manfred nodded.

“Very indistinctly﻿—one could just see the Houses of Parliament dimly, and Downing Street is a jumble of roofs.”

Thery had turned to the work that was engaging his attention. Whatever was his trade he was a deft workman. Somehow he felt that he must do his best for these men. He had been made forcibly aware of their superiority in the last days, he had now an ambition to assert his own skill, his own individuality, and to earn commendation from these men who had made him feel his littleness.

===Chinese===
「那么你看见——？」波伊卡特刚开口。

曼弗雷德点头。

「非常朦胧——勉强能望见议会两院的轮廓，唐宁街则是一堆乱糟糟的屋顶。」

特里已回头忙他手头的活计。且不论他的行当究竟是什么，他确是名灵巧的工匠。不知怎的，他觉得必须为这几个人尽全力。这些天他被迫领教了他们的高明，如今他有一股志气，要显一显自己的手艺、自己的独立人格，要赢得这几个曾令他自觉渺小的人的一句夸赞。

===Original===
Manfred and the others stood aside and watched him in silence. Leon, with a perplexed frown, kept his eyes fixed on the workman’s face, for Leon Gonsalez, scientist, physiognomist (his translation of the *Theologi Physiognomia Humana* of Lequetius is regarded today as the finest), was endeavouring to reconcile the criminal with the artisan.

After a while Thery finished.

“All is now ready,” he said, with a grin of satisfaction; “let me find your minister of state, give me a minute’s speech with him, and the next minute he dies.” His face, repulsive in repose, was now demoniacal. He was like some great bull from his own country made more terrible with the snuffle of blood in his nostrils.

===Chinese===
曼弗雷德等人站在一旁，默然观望。莱昂蹙眉苦思，目光不离工匠的脸——因为莱昂·冈萨雷斯，科学家、相士（他翻译的勒奎提乌斯（Lequetius）《人的神学相面术》（*Theologi Physiognomia Humana*），至今被推为第一），正努力把罪犯与匠人在同一张脸上调和起来。

过了一阵，特里完工了。

「如今万事齐备。」他咧嘴一笑，一脸餍足；「让我寻着你们那位国务大臣，给我一分钟同他说话，下一分钟他就死。」他那张静下来时便惹人厌的脸，此刻已如恶鬼。像他本国的一头巨牛，鼻息里嗅进了血腥，愈发可怖。

===Original===
In strange contrast were the faces of his employers. Not a muscle of either face stirred. There was neither exultation nor remorse in their expressions﻿—only a curious something that creeps into the set face of the judge as he pronounces the dread sentence of the law. Thery saw that something, and it froze him to his very marrow.

He threw out his hands as if to ward them off.

“Stop! stop!” he shouted; “don’t look like that, in the name of God﻿—don’t, don’t!” He covered his face with shaking hands.

===Chinese===
他的雇主们的脸恰成奇特的对照。两张脸上没有一块肌肉动过。神情里既无得意，也无悔恨——只有一种古怪的东西，正是法官宣读法律那可怖判决时，悄悄爬上那张绷紧的脸的东西。特里看见了那东西，它直把他冻到了骨髓里。

他猛地伸出双手，像要把什么挡开。

「停下！停下！」他喊道；「看在上帝份上，别那样看——别，别！」他双手发抖，捂住了脸。

===Original===
“Like what, Thery?” asked Leon, softly.

Thery shook his head.

“I cannot say﻿—like the judge at Granada when he says﻿—when he says, ‘Let the thing be done’!”

===Chinese===
「哪样，特里？」莱昂轻声问。

特里摇头。

「我说不上——像格拉纳达的法官，他说——他说：『把事情办了吧』！」

===Original===
“If we look so,” said Manfred harshly, “it is because we are judges﻿—and not alone judges but executioners of our judgment.”

“I thought you would have been pleased,” whimpered Thery.

“You have done well,” said Manfred, gravely.

===Chinese===
「我们若真像那样，」曼弗雷德的声音很冷，「那是因为我们是法官——且不只是法官，还是我们判决的行刑人。」

「我原以为你们会高兴的。」特里呜咽道。

「你干得很好。」曼弗雷德郑重地说。

===Original===
“*Bueno, bueno!*” echoed the others.

“Pray God that we are successful,” added Manfred solemnly, and Thery stared at this strange man in blank amazement.

===Chinese===
「*Bueno, bueno!*（好，好！）」其余两人齐声应道。

「但愿上帝保佑我们成功。」曼弗雷德又庄重地补了一句，特里直勾勾盯着这个怪人，全然呆了。

---
===Original===
Superintendent Falmouth reported to the commissioner that afternoon that all arrangements were now complete for the protection of the threatened minister.

“I’ve filled up 44, Downing Street,” he said; “there’s practically a man in every room. I’ve got four of our best men on the roof, men in the basement, men in the kitchens.”

===Chinese===
当天下午，法尔茅斯警司向总监汇报：保护受威胁大臣的一切部署，业已就绪。

「我把唐宁街 44 号塞满了。」他说；「几乎每个房间都有人。屋顶上派了四个最得力的人，地下室有人，厨房有人。」

===Original===
“What about the servants?” asked the commissioner.

“Sir Philip has brought up his own people from the country, and now there isn’t a person in the house from the private secretary to the doorkeeper whose name and history I do not know from A to Z.”

The commissioner breathed an anxious sigh.

===Chinese===
「仆役那边呢？」总监问。

「菲利普爵士把乡下自己人都带上来了，如今这宅子里从私人秘书到门房，没有一个人的姓名来历，我不掌握得从 A 到 Z。」

总监焦虑地叹了口气。

===Original===
“I shall be very glad when tomorrow is over,” he said. “What are the final arrangements?”

“There has been no change, sir, since we fixed things up the morning Sir Philip came over. He remains at 44 all day tomorrow until half-past eight, goes over to the House at nine to move the reading of the bill, returns at eleven.”

“I have given orders for the traffic to be diverted along the Embankment between a quarter to nine and a quarter after, and the same at eleven,” said the commissioner. “Four closed carriages will drive from Downing Street to the House; Sir Philip will drive down on a motorcar immediately afterwards.”

===Chinese===
「明天过去，我就谢天谢地了。」他说。「最终部署怎么定的？」

「自菲利普爵士搬来那天早上定下之后，就没再变动，先生。明天他全天留在 44 号，到八点半，九点钟去议院动议法案二读，十一点返回。」

「我已下令，八点三刻到九点一刻之间，车辆一律改行河滨大道，十一点那趟同样办理。」总监说。「将有四辆密闭马车从唐宁街驶往议院；菲利普爵士随后即刻乘汽车前往。」

===Original===
There was a rap at the door﻿—the conversation took place in the commissioner’s office﻿—and a police officer entered. He bore a card in his hand, which he laid upon the table.

“Señor Jose di Silva,” read the commissioner, “the Spanish Chief of Police,” he explained to the superintendent. “Show him in, please.”

Señor di Silva, a lithe little man, with a pronounced nose and a beard, greeted the Englishmen with the exaggerated politeness that is peculiar to Spanish official circles.

===Chinese===
门上响起敲击——谈话是在总监办公室进行的——一名警官走进来，手中托着一张名帖，放在桌上。

「何塞·迪席尔瓦先生（Señor Jose di Silva），」总监念道，「西班牙警察总监。」他向警司解释。「请他进来。」

迪席尔瓦先生身量小巧灵活，高鼻，蓄须，以一种西班牙官场特有的过度客气向英国人致意。

===Original===
“I am sorry to bring you over,” said Mr. Commissioner, after he had shaken hands with the visitor and had introduced him to Falmouth; “we thought you might be able to help us in our search for Thery.”

“Luckily I was in Paris,” said the Spaniard; “yes, I know Thery, and I am astounded to find him in such distinguished company. Do I know the Four?”﻿—his shoulders went up to his ears﻿—“who does? I know of them﻿—there was a case at Malaga, you know?﻿ ﻿… Thery is not a good criminal. I was astonished to learn that he had joined the band.”

“By the way,” said the chief, picking up a copy of the police notice that lay on his desk, and running his eye over it, “your people omitted to say﻿—although it really isn’t of very great importance﻿—what is Thery’s trade?”

===Chinese===
「有劳您跑这一趟，」总监先生与来客握手，并把他介绍给法尔茅斯之后说，「我们想，寻访特里的事，您也许帮得上忙。」

「幸好我人在巴黎。」西班牙人说；「是的，我认识特里，发现他混进这般显赫的队伍，我大吃一惊。我认得四义士？」——他的肩膀耸到了耳朵边——「谁认得？我知道他们的名头——马拉加（Malaga）有过一桩案子，知道吧？……特里算不得好罪犯。听说他入了伙，我着实吃惊。」

「顺便问一句，」总监拿起桌上那份警方告示，目光扫过，「贵国方面漏说了一件事——虽说实在无关紧要——特里是干哪一行的？」

===Original===
The Spanish policeman knitted his brow.

“Thery’s trade! Let me remember”; he thought for a moment. “Thery’s trade? I don’t think I know; yet I have an idea that it is something to do with rubber. His first crime was stealing rubber; but if you want to know for certain﻿—”

The commissioner laughed.

“It really isn’t at all important,” he said lightly.

===Chinese===
西班牙警察皱起眉头。

「特里的行当！让我想想。」他想了片刻。「特里的行当？我说不上知道；不过我有点印象，好像同橡胶有关。他头一桩案子就是偷橡胶；不过你们若要问个确凿——」

总监笑了。

「实在无关紧要。」他轻飘飘地说。

## VIII The Messenger of the Four / 八 四义士的信使
===Original===
There was yet another missive to be handed to the doomed minister. In the last he had received there had occurred the sentence: “One more warning you shall receive, and, so that we may be assured it shall not go astray, our next and last message shall be delivered into your hands by one of us in person.”

This passage afforded the police more comfort than had any episode since the beginning of the scare. They placed a curious faith in the honesty of the Four Men; they recognized that these were not ordinary criminals and that their pledge was inviolable. Indeed, had they thought otherwise, the elaborate precautions that they were taking to ensure the safety of Sir Philip would not have been made. The honesty of the Four was their most terrible characteristic.

In this instance it served to raise a faint hope that the men who were setting at defiance the establishment of the law would overreach themselves. The letter conveying this message was the one to which Sir Philip had referred so airily in his conversation with his secretary. It had come by post, bearing the date mark, “Balham, 12:15.”

===Chinese===
那位命在旦夕的大臣，还有最后一封信要收。在他收到的前一封里，有这么一句话：「你还将收到最后一次警告；为确保它不致错投，我们下一封、也是最后一封信，将由我们中的一人亲手交到你手中。」

这段话给警方带来的宽慰，超过了这场风波开始以来的任何一桩事。他们对四义士的诚信怀着一种奇特的信心；他们承认这几位不是寻常罪犯，他们的誓言不可违逆。说实在的，若非这样想，他们为保菲利普爵士周全所布下的种种周密防范，也就不必做了。四义士的诚信，正是他们最可怕的特征。

这一次，这诚信还升起了一线微茫的希望：这伙公然蔑视法律建制的人，或许会聪明反被聪明误。捎来这句话的信，正是菲利普爵士同秘书谈话时那样轻飘飘提到的那封。它是邮局寄来的，盖着「巴尔哈姆，12:15」的日戳。

===Original===
“The question is, shall we keep you absolutely surrounded, so that these men cannot by any possible chance carry out their threat?” asked Superintendent Falmouth, in some perplexity, “or shall we apparently relax our vigilance in order to lure one of the Four to his destruction?”

The question was directed to Sir Philip Ramon as he sat huddled up in the capacious depths of his office chair.

“You want to use me as a bait?” he asked sharply.

===Chinese===
「问题在于，是把你团团围死，叫这几个人无论如何下不了手，」法尔茅斯警司颇为踌躇地问，「还是表面上松一松防备，引四义士之一上钩送死？」

这问题是抛给菲利普·拉蒙爵士的，他正蜷在办公椅宽大的深处。

「你们要拿我当饵？」他厉声问。

===Original===
The detective expostulated.

“Not exactly that, sir; we want to give these men a chance﻿—”

“I understand perfectly,” said the minister, with some show of irritation.

===Chinese===
侦探赶紧分辩。

「不全是那么回事，先生；我们想给这几个人一个机会——」

「我完全明白。」大臣说着，微露愠色。

===Original===
The detective resumed:

“We know now how the infernal machine was smuggled into the House; on the day on which the outrage was committed an old member, Mr. Bascoe, the member for North Torrington, was seen to enter the House.”

“Well?” asked Sir Philip in surprise.

“Mr. Bascoe was never within a hundred miles of the House of Commons on that date,” said the detective quietly. “We might never have found it out, for his name did not appear in the division list. We’ve been working quietly on that House of Commons affair ever since, and it was only a couple of days ago that we made the discovery.”

===Chinese===
侦探续道：

「那台爆炸装置是怎么混进议院的，我们如今知道了；案发那天，有人看见一位老议员，北托灵顿选区的巴斯科先生，进了议院。」

「然后呢？」菲利普爵士诧异地问。

「巴斯科先生那一天压根儿就在离下议院一百英里开外的地方。」侦探平静地说。「我们本可能永远查不出来，因为表决名单上没有他的名字。下议院那桩事，我们一直悄悄在查，直到两三天前才有了这个发现。」

===Original===
Sir Philip sprang from his chair and nervously paced the floor of his room.

“Then they are evidently well acquainted with the conditions of life in England,” he asserted rather than asked.

“Evidently; they’ve got the lay of the land, and that is one of the dangers of the situation.”

===Chinese===
菲利普爵士从椅中一跃而起，焦躁地在屋里踱来踱去。

「这么说，他们对英国的生活底细显然了如指掌。」这与其说是问，不如说是断言。

「显然；他们摸熟了地形，这正是眼下局面的危险之一。」

===Original===
“But,” frowned the other, “you have told me there were no dangers, no real dangers.”

“There is this danger, sir,” replied the detective, eyeing the minister steadily, and dropping his voice as he spoke; “men who are capable of making such disguise are really outside the ordinary run of criminals. I don’t know what their game is, but whatever it is, they are playing it thoroughly. One of them is evidently an artist at that sort of thing, and he’s the man I’m afraid of﻿—today.”

===Chinese===
「可是，」对方皱起眉头，「你告诉过我，没有危险，没有真正的危险。」

「有这一桩，先生。」侦探答道，眼睛紧盯大臣，说话时压低了嗓音；「能做出那种化装的人，实在超出了寻常罪犯的行列。我不知道他们玩的什么把戏，但不管是什么，他们玩得一板一眼。其中一位显然是此道艺术家，我怕的就是他——就今天。」

===Original===
Sir Philip’s head tossed impatiently.

“I am tired of all this, tired of it”﻿—he thrashed the edge of his desk with an open palm﻿—“detectives and disguises and masked murderers until the atmosphere is, for all the world, like that of a melodrama.”

“You must have patience for a day or two,” said the plainspoken officer. The Four Just Men were on the nerves of more people than the foreign minister. “And we have not decided what is to be our plan for this evening,” he added.

===Chinese===
菲利普爵士不耐烦地把头一甩。

「这一切我受够了，受够了」——他一巴掌一巴掌拍着书桌沿——「侦探、化装、蒙面杀手，闹得这空气活脱脱一出情节剧。」

「您还得忍这一两天。」这位心直口快的警官说。四义士刺激着的，可不止外交大臣一个人的神经。「今晚的方案，我们也还没定。」他补了一句。

===Original===
“Do as you like,” said Sir Philip shortly, and then, “Am I to be allowed to go to the House tonight?”

“No; that is not part of the programme,” replied the detective.

Sir Philip stood for a moment in thought.

===Chinese===
「随你们便。」菲利普爵士简短地说，随即又问：「今晚还许我去议院吗？」

「不去；那不在计划之内。」侦探答道。

菲利普爵士站着想了一会儿。

===Original===
“These arrangements; they are kept secret, I suppose?”

“Absolutely.”

“Who knows of them?”

“Yourself, the Commissioner, your secretary, and myself.”

===Chinese===
「这些部署；想来是保密的吧？」

「绝对保密。」

「都有谁知道？」

「您本人、总监、您的秘书，加我。」

===Original===
“And no one else?”

“No one; there is no danger likely to arise from that source. If your safety depended upon the secrecy of it, it would be plain sailing.”

“Have these arrangements been committed to writing?” asked Sir Philip.

“No, sir; nothing has been written: our plans have been settled upon and communicated verbally; even the prime minister does not know.”

===Chinese===
「再没有别人？」

「没有；这一头出不了乱子。您的安危若系在这层保密上，那倒一帆风顺了。」

「这些部署落到纸面了吗？」菲利普爵士问。

「没有，先生；一个字也没写：计划是当面议定、口头传达的；连首相都不知道。」

===Original===
Sir Philip breathed a sigh of relief.

“That is all to the good,” he said, as the detective rose to go.

“I must see the commissioner. I shall be away for less than half an hour; in the meantime I suggest that you do not leave your room,” he said.

===Chinese===
菲利普爵士松了一口气。

「那就好。」侦探起身要走时，他说。

「我得去见总监。前后不到半个钟头；这段时间，奉劝您别离开房间。」他说。

===Original===
Sir Philip followed him to the anteroom, in which sat Hamilton, the secretary.

“I have had an uncomfortable feeling,” said Falmouth, as one of his men approached with a long coat, which he proceeded to help the detective into, “a sort of instinctive feeling this last day or two, that I have been watched and followed, so that I am using a motorcar to convey me from place to place; they can’t follow that without attracting some notice.” He dipped his hand into the pocket and brought out a pair of motor-goggles; he laughed somewhat shamefacedly as he adjusted them. “This is the only disguise I ever adopt, and I might say, Sir Philip,” he added with some regret, “that this is the first time during my twenty-five years of service that I have ever played the fool like a stage detective.”

===Chinese===
菲利普爵士送他到前厅，秘书汉密尔顿（Hamilton）坐在那里。

「这两天我总觉得不自在，」法尔茅斯说；他手下一个人拿了件长外套过来，帮侦探穿上。「一种本能似的感觉，觉得自己被人盯着、跟梢，所以我改乘汽车来回；他们要跟汽车，不惹眼是不行的。」他把手探进口袋，掏出一副风镜；戴上时有点讪讪地笑了。「这是我唯一的化装。还可以说一句，菲利普爵士，」他颇有些遗憾地补道，「这是我当差二十五年头一回像舞台上的侦探那样耍宝。」

===Original===
After Falmouth’s departure the foreign minister returned to his desk. He hated being alone: it frightened him. That there were twoscore detectives within call did not dispel the feeling of loneliness. The terror of the Four was ever with him, and this had so worked upon his nerves that the slightest noise irritated him. He played with the penholder that lay on the desk. He scribbled inconsequently on the blotting-pad before him, and was annoyed to find that the scribbling had taken the form of numbers of figure 4.

Was the bill worth it? Was the sacrifice called for? Was the measure of such importance as to justify the risk? These things he asked himself again and again, and then immediately, What sacrifice? What risk?

“I am taking the consequences too much for granted,” he muttered, throwing aside the pen, and half turning from the writing-table. “There is no certainty that they will keep their words; bah! it is impossible that they should﻿—”

===Chinese===
法尔茅斯走后，外交大臣回到书桌前。他恨独处：独处叫他心惊。哪怕一唤即来四十名侦探，也驱不散那份孤独感。对四义士的恐惧寸步不离，把他的神经磨得一点响动就冒火。他把玩着桌上的笔杆，在面前的吸墨纸上胡乱涂写，写罢一看，恼火地发现，涂出来的竟是一连串的 4。

这法案值得吗？这牺牲是必须的吗？这项措施重要到足以担当这样的风险吗？这些问题他一遍遍问自己，随即又立刻问：什么牺牲？什么风险？

「我把后果想得太笃定了。」他丢开笔，从写字台半侧过身，喃喃道。「他们未必说到做到；呸！他们根本不可能——」

===Original===
There was a knock at the door.

“Hullo! Superintendent,” said the foreign minister as the knocker entered. “Back again already!”

The detective, vigorously brushing the dust from his moustache with a handkerchief, drew an official-looking blue envelope from his pocket.

“I thought I had better leave this in your care,” he said, dropping his voice; “it occurred to me just after I had left; accidents happen, you know.”

===Chinese===
门上响起敲击。

「哟！警司。」敲门人进来时，外交大臣说。「这么快就回来了！」

侦探正用手帕使劲掸髭上的尘，闻言从口袋里抽出一只官样十足的蓝信封。

「我想还是把它交给您保管的好。」他压低声音说；「刚一出门就想起这事；您知道，意外总是有的。」

===Original===
The minister took the document.

“What is it?” he asked.

“It is something which would mean absolute disaster for me if by chance it was found in my possession,” said the detective, turning to go.

“What am I to do with it?”

“You would greatly oblige me by putting it in your desk until I return”; and the detective stepped into the anteroom, closed the door behind him and, acknowledging the salute of the plainclothes officer who guarded the outer door, passed to the motorcar that waited him.

===Chinese===
大臣接过那件东西。

「这是什么？」他问。

「万一在我身上被搜出来，对我来说就是灭顶之灾的东西。」侦探说着，转身要走。

「我怎么处置它？」

「劳驾放进您书桌里，等我回来取」；侦探跨进前厅，掩上身后的门，向守着外门的便衣警官回了礼，走向候着他的汽车。

===Original===
Sir Philip looked at the envelope with a puzzled frown.

It bore the superscription “Confidential” and the address, “Department A. C.I.D., Scotland Yard.”

“Some confidential report,” thought Sir Philip, and an angry doubt as to the possibility of its containing particulars of the police arrangements for his safety filled his mind. He had hit by accident upon the truth had he but known. The envelope contained those particulars.

===Chinese===
菲利普爵士皱着眉，困惑地端详这只信封。

信封上标着「机密」，地址是「苏格兰场刑事调查部 A 科」。

「某份机密报告吧。」菲利普爵士想着，一股恼怒的疑云涌上心头：它会不会装着警方为他安全所作部署的细节。他其实瞎猫碰上死耗子，正猜中了真相——可惜他自己不知道。信封里装的正是那些细节。

===Original===
He placed the letter in a drawer of his desk and drew some papers toward him. They were copies of the Bill for the passage of which he was daring so much. It was not a long document. The clauses were few in number, the objects, briefly described in the preamble, were tersely defined. There was no fear of this bill failing to pass on the morrow. The Government’s majority was assured. Men had been brought back to town, stragglers had been whipped in, prayers and threats alike had assisted in concentrating the rapidly dwindling strength of the administration on this one effort of legislation; and what the frantic entreaties of the whips had failed to secure, curiosity had accomplished, for members of both parties were hurrying to town to be present at a scene which might perhaps be history, and, as many feared, tragedy.

As Sir Philip conned the paper he mechanically formed in his mind the line of attack﻿—for, tragedy or none, the bill struck at too many interests in the House to allow of its passage without a stormy debate. He was a master of dialectics, a brilliant casuist, a coiner of phrases that stuck and stung. There was nothing for him to fear in the debate. If only﻿—. It hurt him to think of the Four Just Men, not so much because they threatened his life﻿—he had gone past that﻿—but the mere thought that there had come a new factor into his calculations, a new and a terrifying force, that could not be argued down or brushed aside with an acid jest, or intrigued against, or adjusted by any parliamentary method. He did not think of compromise. The possibility of making terms with his enemy never once entered his head.

===Chinese===
他把信放进书桌抽屉，把几份文件拉到面前。那是法案的誊本——为把它送过关，他押上了这么多。文件不长。条款寥寥，序言里简述的宗旨，界定得一清二楚。这法案明天通不过，是无须担心的。政府的多数稳操在手。人被接回了城里，散兵游勇被鞭子赶拢，恳求与威吓齐上，把政府那日渐消耗的兵力，尽数压在这一桩立法上；党鞭们声泪俱下的央告办不到的，好奇心办到了——两党议员都在赶回城里，要亲眼看一场说不定要载入史册、也如许多人所惧、要变成惨剧的好戏。

菲利普爵士细读文卷时，心里已机械地摆好了攻防的阵势——因为无论惨剧与否，这法案触动院内太多利益，不经过一场疾风骤雨的辩论，休想过关。他是辩证法大师，出色的诡辩家，铸得出又黏人又扎人的警句。辩论一道，他无所畏惧。但愿——。想到四义士，他便如针在心；倒不全是他们要他的命——那一关他早过了——而是单凭这个念头：他的算盘里闯进了一个新变量，一股全新而骇人的力量，骂不倒，一句刻薄俏皮话打发不了，暗中设局扳不动，任何议会手段也调和不来。他不想妥协。同敌人讲和的可能，从未在他脑子里出现过一次。

===Original===
“I’ll go through with it!” he cried, not once but a score of times; “I’ll go through with it!” and now, as the moment grew nearer to hand, his determination to try conclusions with this new world-force grew stronger than ever.

The telephone at his elbow purred﻿—he was sitting at his desk with his head on his hands﻿—and he took the receiver. The voice of his house steward reminded him that he had arranged to give instructions for the closing of the house in Portland Place. For two or three days, or until this terror had subsided, he intended that his house should be empty. He would not risk the lives of his servants. If the Four intended to carry out their plan they would run no risks of failure, and if the method they employed were a bomb, then, to make assurance doubly sure, an explosion at Downing Street might well synchronize with an outrage at Portland Place.

===Chinese===
「我一定办到底！」他喊道，喊了一遍又一遍；「一定办到底！」如今，时辰越近，他要同这股新的世界力量一决雌雄的决心，越是坚定。

手边的电话咕噜咕噜响了——他正坐在书桌前，头支在手上——他拿起听筒。管家的话音提醒他，他约好了交代封闭波特兰街宅子的事。两三天里，或者说直到这恐怖平息，他都要宅子空着。他不愿拿仆人的性命冒险。四义士若要实行计划，是不会留下失手的隐患的；倘若他们用的法子是炸弹，那么为保险起见，唐宁街的一声爆炸，很可能与波特兰街的一场暴行同时落地。

===Original===
He had finished his talk, and was replacing the receiver when a knock at the door heralded the entry of the detective.

He looked anxiously at the minister.

“Nobody been, sir?” he asked.

===Chinese===
他通话完毕，正把听筒放回去，门上一声敲击，通报了侦探进来。

他急切地望了望大臣。

「没来过人吧，先生？」他问。

===Original===
Sir Philip smiled.

“If by that you mean have the Four delivered their ultimatum in person, I can comfort your mind﻿—they have not.”

The detective’s face was evidence of his relief.

“Thank Heaven!” he said fervently. “I had an awful dread that whilst I was away something would happen. But I have news for you, sir.”

===Chinese===
菲利普爵士笑了。

「你要是问四义士有没有亲自送来最后通牒，那我可以让你安心——没有。」

侦探脸上写满了如释重负。

「谢天谢地！」他虔诚地说。「我一直怕得要死，怕我不在这工夫出事。不过，先生，我给您带来了消息。」

===Original===
“Indeed!”

“Yes, sir, the commissioner has received a long cable from America. Since the two murders in that country one of Pinkerton’s men has been engaged in collecting data. For years he has been piecing together the scrappy evidence he has been able to secure, and this is his cablegram.” The detective drew a paper from his pocket and, spreading it on the desk, read:

===Chinese===
「哦！」

「是，先生，总监收到美国来的一封长电。自从那两桩命案之后，平克顿（Pinkerton）手下的一名探员一直在收集材料。多年来他把弄到手的零星证据一点点拼起来，这就是他发来的电文。」侦探从口袋里抽出一张纸，摊在桌上，念道：

===Original===
> Warn Ramon that the Four do not go outside their promise. If they have threatened to kill in a certain manner at a certain time they will be punctual. We have proof of this characteristic. After Anderson’s death small memorandum book was discovered outside window of room evidently dropped. Book was empty save for three pages, which were filled with neatly written memoranda headed “Six methods of execution.” It was initialed C. (third letter in alphabet). Warn Ramon against following: drinking coffee in any form, opening letters or parcels, using soap that has not been manufactured under eye of trustworthy agent, sitting in any room other than that occupied day and night by police officer. Examine his bedroom; see if there is any method by which heavy gases can be introduced. We are sending two men by *Lucania* to watch.

===Chinese===
> 告诫拉蒙：四义士行事不出其诺言一步。若彼等曾威胁以特定方式于特定时刻杀人，彼等必准时。此特性吾方握有实证。安德森死后，其室外窗下拾得小记事簿一册，显系遗落。簿中除三页外皆空白，三页上写满工整摘记，题为「行刑六法」。署首字母 C.（字母表第三位）。告诫拉蒙勿犯下列诸事：饮用任何形式的咖啡；拆启信件或包裹；使用非经可信之人监制之肥皂；坐卧于非警察日夜驻守之房间。检查其卧室；查明有无引入重气体之途径。吾方现派两人乘「卢卡尼亚号」（*Lucania*）前来监视。

===Original===
